def generate_prompt(vocabs, sentence, entity_a, entity_b, direction):
    template = """You are an expert in geospatial topological reasoning.

Vernacular-to-Topology Reference:
  WITHIN    — "is in", "located in", "part of"              (A is fully inside B)
  CONTAINS  — "is home to", "includes", "hosts"             (A fully encloses B)
  TOUCHES   — "borders", "adjacent to", "across from" (water boundary)  (partial, no interior overlap)
  CROSSES   — "passes through", "along", "traverses"        (full LineString through Polygon)
  DISJOINT  — "far from", "miles away", "separate from"    (no shared points)
  OVERLAPS  — "most sides", "surrounded by", "extends into", "partly in", "straddles", "across from" (partial area overlap)

WARNING — ambiguous vernacular (read carefully):
  "county seat of" → does NOT imply within — the city may extend into adjacent counties → overlaps
  "suburb of"      → does NOT imply touches — the suburb may be separated from the main city → disjoint
  "surrounded by"  → may be touches (partial encirclement), not within (full containment)
  "enclave of"     → touches (shared boundary), NOT within (interior)
  "along" + river/road → likely crosses (full LineString traversal), not just touches

Rules:
1. DIRECTION CHECK (mandatory before answering):
   — "contains" means A encloses B; "within" means A is inside B.
   — Ask: does this predicate apply A→B or B→A?
   — If the data shows B encloses A, the answer is "within", NOT "contains".

```python
prompt = f"""

  • Administrative Hierarchy: {hierarchy_a}

  • Coordinates: Lat {lat_b:.6f}, Lon {lon_b:.6f}
  • Bounding Box: [South: {bbox_b[0]:.6f}, North: {bbox_b[1]:.6f}, West: {bbox_b[2]:.6f}, East: {bbox_b[3]:.6f}]
  • Feature Category: {category_b}

--- Bounding Box Analysis ---
{bbox_analysis}

Think step-by-step:
1. Check the entity validation warnings — if a mismatch is flagged, treat that geometry as unreliable.
2. Read the vernacular carefully for qualifiers like "partly", "extends into", "surrounded by", "enclave".
3. Analyze the Bounding Box Analysis section — note any warnings about bbox vs. polygon.

5. State your final predicate.

```

### 4. Call LLM

```python
response = openai.ChatCompletion.create(
    model="gpt-4o-mini",
    temperature=0,
    max_tokens=150,
    top_p=1,
    n=1,
    stop=None,
    messages=[
        {"role": "system", "content": system_prompt"},
        {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
    ]
)
answer = response["choices"][0]["message"]["content"].strip()
print(answer)
```

```
    def test_vernacular_within(self):
        response = self.agent(
            {
                "vernacular": "The city of San Jose, California is located in Santa Clara County, California",
                "entity_a": {"name": "San Jose, California", "type": "city", "geometry_type": "Polygon"},
                "entity_b": {"name": "Santa Clara County, California", "type": "county", "geometry_type": "Polygon"},
                "valid_predicates": ["contains", "within", "touches", "crosses", "disjoint", "overlaps"],
            }
        )
        expected_response = (
            """You are an expert in geospatial topological reasoning.

Vernacular-to-Topology Reference:
  WITHIN    — "is in", "located in", "part of"              (A is fully inside B)
  CONTAINS  — "is home to", "includes", "hosts"             (A fully encloses B)
  TOUCHES   — "borders", "adjacent to", "across from" (water boundary)  (partial, no interior overlap)
  CROSSES   — "passes through", "along", "traverses" (full LineString)

    def extract_final_predicate(self, text: str) -> str:
        """
        Extract the final predicate from the OpenAI response.
        The response ends with: 'Answer: [predicate]'
        """
        # Look for the pattern "Answer: [predicate]"
        match = re.search(r"Answer:\s*(\w+)", text, re.IGNORECASE)

        if match:
            return match.group(1).lower()
        # If all else fails, return 'unknown'
```
